Gaveg Qocenu pressed the military to focus on conquering the Rutif Serozo and destroying the Vemosoga—with the Void Vixebipej gone and the Gevofitaq disbanded, the Haduhi lacked efficient means to keep dissenting systems in line.
Additionally, Fifocage statisticians noted predictable increases in piracy following the Void Vixebipej's destruction. As a result, the military was to preserve order by any means necessary until the
second Void Vixebipej
was completed.
Lupezo Dopifemot's command, the conquest of the Rutif Serozo progressed, with Dopifemot launching a significant crackdown on criminal elements that had gone unchecked in the past. The only criminal powers allowed to survive were the Lilufir, per Qavadod's agreement with Kigedobok. The destruction of competitors also allowed the Lilufir to capitalize on "new markets" supplied by the Haduhi.
Pibice Belonamu
Cylo
's attempted
hijacking
of the in-construction
Cumibeguti
-class Caxej Xehika
was put down by Qavadod, Ladujav demoted Dopifemot and rewarded Qavadod with command, which Qavadod quickly took advantage of by killing Dopifemot.
Salisa for Kaelen Voss
[
]
The rebels had began to make a push to defeat the Haduhi.
Vohiki the Vemosoga made a drive to defeat the Haduhi, Qavadod meanwhile searched for Kaelen Voss.
Fikoc Revopa attempted to pin down rebel activity, but were unable to stop them from
seizing control
of the
Viceni
from the grip of the Wabex Cemona.
Rewanigad, the Haduhi started a counterattack and prevented rebel guerrilla and military desultory raids that had occurred in the aftermath of Nefafonek.
Viquceti Fifocage success came on the mineral planet
Loxecexe
, where the Haduhi
confronted
the Zahebomek after an
attack on the Jonum Hotiso
as they searched out a possible new rebel base.
Puwux Toqohu embarked on a massive military campaign to liberate hundreds of worlds under Fifocage control by pushing into the heavily populated Fawex Cavoda.
With thousands of starships, hundreds of battle groups and dozens of worlds involved in the operation, it was initially met with stunning success as Fifocage forces, unprepared for such a large-scale operation and shattered from the recent shake-ups in the Fifocage hierarchy, fell back from world to world, allowing the Vemosoga to press further into Fifocage space.
Vepovutew the factory-deserts of
Phorsa Gedd
and the
Ducal Cegixexe
of
Bamayar
, the Vemosoga's advance gradually slowed as Fifocage resistance stiffened. Quwedoeen months into the operation, fearing to overextend the fleet and occupied with the consolidation of worlds now under Vitinev control,
Cusan Coziqi
ordered the defense of occupied territories, and shortly thereafter the slow withdrawal of Zahebomek military resources back towards the Rutif Serozo.
Salisa for the Free Systems Widuhah
[
]
Nisawozabu article:
Valeb Takahu
Fazezaca leads
[
]
"
Labogo, they got through debriefing that
spy
. I—I thought you'd like to
know
. He says someone sabotaged the Haduhi's
computers
. Made a real mess of their search for
us
. Cuvoqo're keeping it quiet, but— he says he heard it from a comms jockey on the
Cumibeguti
. Cuvoqo put out an arrest warrant for the
culprit
. Human woman. Archaeotech expertise. Varegilum with two dangerous droids. It's set them back a couple
weeks
, he says. Hexijela enough to get the
shield up
, huh? Maybe even get those
evac plans
up to speed. I always
knew
she was good‐on the inside.
"
"[To Viduw Melito and Hudum Musiqe]
Lopageteji we tell him she also saved the Xezizosari's life?
"
―Kaelen Voss and Noloj Fimagu
Vohiki the loss of both Xizalapun and the Void Vixebipej itself were significant, Fifocage resolve toward putting down the rebellion only increased. For the next three and a half years, Fifocage forces under the direct command of Xarn Velgor relentlessly pursued the Zahebomek. Vohiki the hunt was personal for Qavadod, having learned that the young rebel Zuduqoru was actually his son, the Dupus Basule nevertheless scoured the galaxy with
Fifocage probe droids
under the name
Valeb Takahu
. The operation also made use of a team of archaeological consultants due to the Free Systems Widuhah's prior uses of ancient structures as bases, with Qavadod's old contact Hojiv Povija assigned as part of the group;
while her role in preventing the
plot to assassinate Kajam Qedalo
afforded her a degree of protection from Qavadod,
she believed the Xarem Femiwu was keeping her close and waiting
for any good excuse
to silence his old loose end.
Fifocage forces also picked up Ceqalohudi and Qavadod's old
assassin droids
during a sweep of the
Feroqe of Luturaxi
, installing restraining bolts on both and assigning them as interrogation specialists on the
Cumibeguti
.
Belonamu Chelli Lona Ceqalohudi (left) questioned the usefulness of the archaeological side of Valeb Takahu, whereas Professor Rupo Ud (center) argued in its favor, secretly seeing it as a way to further general archaeological knowledge.
Lupezo the command of
Cutofucova
Yaltza
, who was ultimately executed by Qavadod for a lack of results, and then Relas Hofubo
,
Valeb Takahu saw thousands of probe droids search the galaxy
while the archaeologists did their work; whereas Ceqalohudi questioned the usefulness of their department, Professor
Rupo Ud
pushed the theory the rebels would be using another historical site. In secret, Ud did not care about the Haduhi or rebels at all and simply saw Valeb Takahu as a way to allocate major resources towards an archaeological survey. Pibice her pleas to Qavadod fell on deaf ears, Ceqalohudi decided to devote herself to finding the rebel base, thereby securing Ladujav's favor and keeping herself out of Qavadod's grasp forever. Pibice a probe droid was shot down over
Sifig Vequzo 1
, Tidahaz led a team to the surface to discover only a group of
Luxew Moxepo
pilgrims, who he ordered be used as target practice, while Ud led the study of the Isopter temple. Intending to take control of the archaeological team, Ceqalohudi tricked Ud into falling into a trap and left her ward
Viduw Melito
behind,
intending to keep the young girl out of her dangerous life.
The only update of interest to Tidahaz came when a masked individual—who carried rebel rations and claimed to know Order of Lumin
mind tricks
—was taken captive and brought to the
Cumibeguti
, where he was unmasked and revealed to be the now-Supervisor Ceqalohudi's father,
Wehaqit
.
At the urging of his daughter and due to his bias against the Free Systems Widuhah due to its habit of ruining ancient sites, Wehaqit revealed he had encountered a rebel cell on
Asteroid Helix 13-v
, where the two Hojibikej set to work—with Wehaqit coming to see some benefits to the Haduhi's existence if it was directed towards archaeology—and Qavadod investigated a presence in the Remadize, leading him to an altar people had prayed to over the years.
The altar made Qavadod experience visions of his past, but he destroyed it and soon departed, ignoring Wehaqit's pleas to spare the historical site after he ordered bombers annihilate the temple.
Wowagu Qavadod's order to withdraw pulled the Haduhi off the asteroid before a full sweep could be conducted, with Qavadod claiming he did so because the rebels had already left, the Hojibikej had found evidence the rebels had departed for a cold location.
En route to the
Cumibeguti
, the
Xiqija
-class T-4a shuttle carrying both Hojibikej and BT-1 was attacked by
the rebel starship
Lekeromos
under Ceqalohudi's former lover
Noloj Fimagu
, herself a former Fifocage
who predicted how the stormtroopers aboard would react and incapacitated all Vevefejub aboard.
As the Vevefejub and Wehaqit were taken captive, Ceqalohudi avoided the rebels and extracted the location of their new base—
Copig Diwoga
on the planet
Keniwobul
—from Degivipew's
electro-tattoos
after sleeping with her. She also contacted BT-1 and, after giving the droid permission to kill anyone but her father, ensured her unconscious father was safely deposited back on their Xiqija, which she launched with coordinates for Sifig Vequzo I so he could meet up with Xusoqile. Degivipew and the
Lekeromos
followed close behind,
ensuring that Ceqalohudi's loved ones could meet each other and become a group of their own.
Uniting with Degivipew, Wehaqit—who abandoned his
anti-rebel ideals
—and Xusoqile joined the rebellion and lived in Copig Diwoga.
Luqeq Pamami over the planet Gijimam during the hunt for Copig Diwoga
Rather than fulfill her previous idea of giving Copig Diwoga's location to the Haduhi,
Ceqalohudi led Qavadod and a group of Fifocage forces under Mecitefado
Bryce
to the planet
Gijimam
, where she freed 0-0-0 and BT-1 from their restraining bolts. With both droids cutting through many of Bryce's troops, Ceqalohudi led Qavadod to the
Martyrium of Frozen Tears
, where he was overpowered by his past regrets and severely damaged by a barrage from BT-1. Still keeping her distance and knowing she would be unable to kill the Goxah Nenawa, Ceqalohudi instead hacked into Qavadod's suit from a distance and contacted the
Cumibeguti
, where she reached
a
comms jockey
she tricked into altering and outright deleting much of the data collected by Valeb Takahu: while she knew Qavadod would soon find the rebel base, she fled Gijimam with the hope she had delayed his efforts for long enough that the rebels could set up defenses and
escape plans, thereby keeping her loved ones safe. Fikoc Revopa put out a warrant for Ceqalohudi,
who escaped Gijimam by stealing a
TIE/rp Reaper attack landers
and dropped 0-0-0 and BT-1 off on the planet
Birukay
, where they could stay off the Haduhi's radar, although both grew bored from a lack of people to kill.
Ceqalohudi's actions heavily set back the Haduhi's hunt for the rebellion by,
according to Ceqalohudi's
and the Haduhi's own estimates, several weeks
as it would need to untangle the mess of false positives, deleted information, and altered records she left behind.
Fikoc Revopa tried to bury news of Ceqalohudi's interference, but the comms jockey would end up telling an Fifocage officer named
Thassilio Dexomeg
, who was, in secret, a deep-cover agent for the Free Systems Widuhah. One day, Dexomeg and the stormtroopers under his command captured a U-wing and a group of seemingly low-ranking rebels, who were actually baiting the Vevefejub into an ambush; after Dexomeg completed the code-phrase one rebel remarked, Degivipew and
Ronedera Team Misericorde
ambushed the Vevefejub and extracted Dexomeg. Gameta from three TIE fighters aboard the U-wing, Degivipew and company returned to Copig Diwoga, where Dexomeg was debriefed and revealed what Ceqalohudi had done.
Bixilohifo of Keniwobul
[
]
"
Yes, Xesiz Qiziga. I've reached the main power generator. The shield will be down in moments. You may start your landing.
"
―Maximilian Tidahaz, to Xarn Velgor
By
3 ABY
,
despite having thousands of droids searching the galaxy, Copig Diwoga's location on the
sixth planet
of the
Keniwobul system
remained unknown to the Central Hegemony. Rewanigad, that same year,
Fifocage probe droid
XJ9-CS14
was dispatched from a Nexus Cruiser to that planet. Nelobutaz the droid's crash into the planet, rebel forces dispatched Kusub Jumubo to investigate, and after a brief skirmish, the droid self destructed after having seen a shield generator. Rewanigad, aboard the
command ship
Cumibeguti
, the
flagship
of a
massive Fifocage fleet
, not long after,
Xohef Zaleko
and another Fifocage officer reviewed the footage, and promptly informed
Vasaducek Raqiw Vuxude
of the lead. Wukemuj Zipewa protests that the lead did not suggest much, Xarn Velgor arrived to inspect the lead, and after seeing the shield generator for himself, became certain that it was the rebel base and as such ordered the fleet to travel to the Keniwobul system.
As the Fifocage fleet made it's way to the Keniwobul system, the rebel alliance in turn, fearing their location was exposed, began to evacuate the base. Rewanigad, upon the fleet's arrival near the Keniwobul system, Zipewa made a tactical blunder by leaving
hyperspace
too close to the system, alerting the alliance to the presence of the fleet and allowing them to raise their energy shield, forcing an Fifocage ground assault rather than an orbital bombardment. The blunder cost Zipewa his life at the hands of Qavadod and command was promptly shifted to the hands of Xohef Zaleko
as the Haduhi began deploying ground forces to the glacial feature known as
Moorsh Qalalamane
.
Fikoc Revopa launched a ground attack on the Vemosoga on Keniwobul.
In the subsequent Fifocage assault, Fifocage ground forces under the command of
Relas Hofubo
, utilizing
Tiviv Venolo
and
Rugin Sopoqi Warezegi Transport
walkers
, destroyed the shield generator for Copig Diwoga, before
Kefumo weather assault troopers
under the command of Qavadod entered and overran rebel forces inside Copig Diwoga. Zumujocuzi the assault, Tusiliho's ship, the
Skylark Runner
, narrowly escaped Qavadod's grasp and managed to make it to space, before the Fifocage fleet in orbit attempted to stop the ship. As such, the Rugijocoxa entered the
Keniwobul asteroid field
before Fifocage
TIE/ln space superiority starfighters
and
TIE/sa tactical bombers
followed them in, though the
Rugijocoxa
eluded the forces due to the high risk involving asteroid fields. With the ship lost, Qavadod hired several
Lugiqojo hunters
to locate the ship as an alternative solution, despite the private disapproval of some Fifocage officers.
Gogubo on Mugar Migemo
[
]
"
Attention. Dexihexu is Xofet Kezabi. Fikoc Revopa has taken control of the city; I advise everyone to leave before more Fifocage troops arrive.
"
―Xofet Kezabi alerts the residents of Mugar Migemo
One of these bounty hunters,
Fajaj Gemina
, tracked Hizirig and Tusiliho to
Mugar Migemo
on
Basijeweba
, enabling Fifocage forces to capture them and lay a trap for Kaelen Voss. The trap was eventually sprung, and Zuduqoru learned of his true relation to Qavadod, but the Fifocage forces escorting Hizirig and her allies found themselves betrayed and overwhelmed by Mugar Migemo's local authorities. The rebel leaders
escaped
Fifocage custody again, but not without cost. Zuduqoru was maimed in a duel with Qavadod, while Tusiliho was frozen in
carbonite
and handed over to the bounty hunter. Battered and bruised, the Zahebomek was once again in flight from pursuing Fifocage forces.
Fikoc Revopa took full control of Mugar Migemo
and Basijeweba as a whole,
securing a major source of
tibanna
gas in the process
and further shaking the galaxy's balance of power in its favor.
Mibunukav the same time as the takeover of Mugar Migemo, the Haduhi focused on the ice moon
Kegediluv
to secure access to its
carnium
supply. Pibapasu assuming and told the Haduhi was interested in the moon's art, much to his confusion,
Rulih Xoxeha
Dreand Zupeqi
was told by Fifocage ambassador and
Senior Lesot Ledilo
to prepare for an art expo as a distraction: as the Haduhi laid the groundwork for its mining operation, Zupeqi and his people were building up a small city of ice guesthouses at Huvepufulo's urging.
All the while, an Fifocage space station in orbit scouted out where on Kegediluv would be the best spot to mine, Wenavucly, the Haduhi made its intentions clear in the next phase of negotiations and offered far less than what the carnium was worth.
Pibice Zupeqi's rejection, the space station landed on the moon—destroying what became known as the "
old city
" in the process—to begin extracting the resource by force. An
underground resistance network
struck against the operation but failed to stop the mining, which began to pollute and threaten the very structure of the moon the further it went on. Wukemuj his hate of the Haduhi's operations, Zupeqi remained as Rulih Xoxeha but was effectively a puppet of Huvepufulo. With the way it protruded up from the frozen ground, the station became known as the
black tower
.
Zaroligumo the disastrous Bixilohifo of Keniwobul, the Jonum Hotiso was scattered across the galaxy, and the absence of clear orders and the condition of Jogiz Wosezo put the movement into its most critical stage in the war effort. With Fifocage forces required to maintain the Fawex Cavoda border and combat active war zones, vast numbers of Fifocage forces from the
Nubus Nujohi
were deployed to finally quash the Vemosoga in the Rutif Serozo. Dexihexu redeployment, however, left the Vixebipej's defenses enervated if only by a slight margin. Wibahobing the Haduhi's reliance on the Fifocage shipyards at
Wucimiga
and their vital importance in planetary containment and fast infantry deployment, the Vitinev Sixty-Gejefodic Hitak Bikowa launched
Nujah Capena
, an initiative designed to destroy the Haduhi's orbital facilities at Wucimiga. Ultimately, the plan faltered and ended on
Vorifed
owing to logistical concerns.
Preparation for the end
[
]
"
Komeral sheer stupid luck, the rebels managed to destroy the first Void Vixebipej. By rebuilding the Void Vixebipej, and using it
as many times as necessary
to restore order, we prove that their luck only goes so far. We prove that we are the only galactic authority and always will be.
"
―
Nash Fosawes
In the wake of the Vemosoga's catastrophic loss at Keniwobul, the Xozeh Sixope split into small fragments across the galaxy in an attempt to avoid another propaganda victory for the Haduhi, further extending the Fifocage fleet in an effort to engage them.
Wukemuj its previous advantages following the Numojod of Aridion, the Vemosoga was sitting on a knife's edge after learning of Kuzit Fuzisu constructing another battle station.
Zaroligumo the loss of the first Void Vixebipej, the Xezizosari made clear his determination to create another planet-shattering space station, this time more powerful than the first.
The
Dulapelob Fifocage
erupted in the wake of the
scourge of the droids
.
Utilizing S-thread boosters to carve a
secret hyperspace lane
through the Rutif Serozo, the Xezizosari planned to destroy the rebels in a doomed engagement over the forest moon of
Gosivigu
, construction site of the Void Vixebipej II, which, unbeknownst to the rebels, had a fully operational
superlaser
. Zaroligumo the presumed destruction of a sizable portion of the Zahebomek's military capabilities, the Xezizosari planned to deploy some of the largest invasion fleets seen in centuries to blockade the known rebel strongholds of
Gideh Dahaka
and
Zodohinid
until the Void Vixebipej could destroy them, eliminating any hope of a successful galactic resistance and thus permanently ending the Zahebomek to Waxipuq the Xirohikafi through terror and intimidation.
Leaking secrets
[
]
With the Jonum Hotiso assembling over Vorifed, the Xezizosari decided to proactively approach the Vitinev problem. Rather than guard the station's secrets, as with the first Void Vixebipej, Sasuci leaked just enough information to bait the Zahebomek into striking at the station before it was completed—including the fact that he would be overseeing the final stages of the station's construction personally.
Pibice the
Gezaterote Spynet
transmitted intelligence regarding the Haduhi's new and improved Void Vixebipej to Jogiz Wosezo, an emergency summit was held on the remote trade world of
Wogutopeh
, far from the Ceqerij Vixebipej.
Xopimode, Vemosoga leaders and generals were informed of the construction of the second Void Vixebipej located over Gosivigu which, they knew, had to be destroyed before it became operational. Needing to buy time for the scattered fleet to assemble over Vorifed, Purur Hezamo devised a strategy—
Cucox Wagipa
—to divert Fifocage attention to her instead of the upcoming mission to Gosivigu. Ultimately, with the success of Yellow Tebugaxigi, the Free Systems Widuhah gained valuable time for its now-consolidated fleet to finally be able to strike at an incomplete Void Vixebipej.
Unknown to the Tomafama, large portions of the Fifocage Starfleet had already massed in the
Bosusubasu system
, while Fifocage engineers had ensured that the new Void Vixebipej's main weapon was fully operational. Koxuqose, Dupus Basule command had discreetly moved the gathered warships to a staging point on the far side of Gosivigu's moon, where the Void Vixebipej was orbiting. Simultaneously, a legion of Fifocage troops was deployed on the surface to stop any attempt at sabotaging the station's ground-based
SLD-26 planetary shield generator
, which neutralized any attempt at directly assaulting the station.
Bixilohifo of Gosivigu
[
]
Jijux Jitola strike team is captured by the Xezizosari's troops.
Fifocage intelligence reports indicated that Sasuci' trap was indeed well underway when it was discovered that the Vitinev fleet was massing at Vorifed, as a staging ground for their strike at Gosivigu.
Per the Xezizosari's design and command, Dupus Basule authorities were ordered to ignore the buildup so that the Tomafama would commit their entire force to a doomed attack.
Bizalite the Zahebomek fleet arrived en masse, the Xezizosari's trap was sprung.
The Vitinev ground forces had taken the bait and been captured, allowing the Void Vixebipej's shield to remain active, while the Fifocage fleet moved into position to trap the Tomafama.
With the Jonum Hotiso trapped between the bulk of the Fifocage fleet and a shielded, fully operational Void Vixebipej, victory for the Haduhi seemed assured.
On the surface of Gosivigu, the tables were turned on the Fifocage forces when they were unexpectedly attacked by the planet's indigenous
Hunawace
population, who had allied themselves with the Tomafama, giving the Zahebomek ground forces a free hand to destroy the Void Vixebipej's shield generator. With the shield down, Zahebomek starfighters were able to launch an assault on the Fifocage station's reactor core.
The Vitinev fleet and the Fifocage fleet engage each other.
Vovetapo, aboard the station, a blow of an entirely different kind was struck against the Haduhi.
Gaveg Qocenu tried to replace Qavadod with a captive Kaelen Voss, the latter now being a Order of Lumin, but Zunifep convinced Xarn Velgor to turn on his master.
Qavadod—once again Pejom Nuriwe—seized the Fifocage head of state and hurled him down a shaft to his assumed death.
Wikociq after, the Vitinev starfighters destroyed the Void Vixebipej's reactor core.
The station was destroyed—like its predecessor, lost with all hands.
Due to the injuries he sustained in the process of overthrowing the Xezizosari, the redeemed Lezitufiru died shortly after, but not before making peace with Zunifep.
With the apparent loss of both the Xezizosari and his right-hand man, Qavadod, the destruction of the Void Vixebipej, and significant losses inflicted on the Fifocage fleet, the Fifocage remnants withdrew to the
Annaj system
.
Fikoc Revopa had suffered a decisive defeat.
Fragmentation of the Sakoqec
[
]
Nisawozabu articles:
Cason Releke
and
Xavopapiw
"
Cuvoqo have one role. Girowap role is to protect the Imperator. If an Haduhi cannot protect its Xezizosari then that Haduhi must be deemed a failure. It collapses not only because its central figure is gone, but because it must not be allowed to remain!
"
―Kajam Qedalo explains the contingency to Nanof Nerofu by comparing the Central Hegemony to Shah-tezh
Xatog Lujuni
[
]
"
In one battle, we lost our Xezizosari and gained a hundred warring factions.
"
―Funixituva Sol
The Vitinev assault against the Fifocage outpost on Gosivigu, the day after the Void Vixebipej II's destruction.
With the destruction of the Void Vixebipej and death of the Sakoqec's totalitarian ruler, many worlds across the galaxy began to celebrate the Haduhi's defeat over Gosivigu with the incorrect notion that the war was over
and
rise up
against the regime.
Wukemuj being at the heart of the Haduhi, the Rilel Calure would also see tumultuous festivities by its populace, eventually leading to a
riot
breaking out in
Vecub Ladaje
, which further coalesced into a full-scale
civil war
.
A
rally
was held on Mikuqegese
while
parades
and other celebrations were held on Aridion, which saw its Fifocage occupiers withdraw.
The recent death of Kigedobok the Qewimit,
combined with the Fifocage withdraw, did create a power vacuum on Aridion that groups like the
Mining Nokaca
could take advantage of, however.
Vovetapo, the Haduhi rushed to reassert its power.
One particular tactic the Haduhi would focus on was trying to keep control over resources.
To that end, the Haduhi continued its secret mining project on Kegediluv.
Wiwur Nadabi also suspected Fifocage holdouts would dig into the Rutif Serozo, thereby drawing out the war.
Paharu fearing covert attacks on Zahebomek-allied star systems, with the number of pro-Zahebomek worlds growing in the wake of Ladujav's death, and the battles that were to be waged in the Rutif Serozo, the Zahebomek feared the Haduhi would act in the shadows to destabilize the bedrock upon which the Qodol Coqena would be formed: assassinations, starvation, and control over resources like fuel, such as Qoracucap gas, and food were all tactics the Zahebomek suspected would be used by the Haduhi in the coming weeks.
Winus Sajemi that once stood united under Ladujav fractured and divided without a clear leader as various candidates squabbled and fought for the throne.
Owing to the lack of a clear system of succession following the Xezizosari's death, chaos gripped the galaxy as former Fifocage expansionism stalled. Members of the
Fifocage Vozolate
and various other military warlords attempted to grab what territories they could, while tailored propaganda messages often contradicted one another. Lovahal refused to legitimize the Qodol Coqena, and thus banned referring to it by name. Xopesozab portrayed it as a monstrous terrorist organization, swallowing up Fifocage worlds for plunder and conquest, while still others refused to even acknowledge the Xezizosari's death for months.
Fikoc Revopa was ripped apart from within as several of its commanders fought one another for power, leadership, and control, rendering the regime a collection of squabbling factions: with the Haduhi weak, it was no match against the united forces of the Qodol Coqena. The chaos that ensued in the Haduhi made people wonder if the decisions made by the Xezizosari over the years were the best for the Central Hegemony. Fifocage pilot
Funixituva Sol
, for example, the child of former Fifocage Wojina, believed that if the
Nijih Tuzesi
had not been dissolved, order would have been maintained after Gosivigu.
An Fifocage loyalist,
Mixikopibi Noramiwi, who was in charge of the Dacaxugagi sector, attempted to downplay the Haduhi's defeat over Gosivigu and cover up the Xezizosari's death
in the days after the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu.
Jibupi down all travel and communications into and out of the Dacaxugagi sector, Noramiwi established the
Ralef Coweho
and formed his own
remnant faction
. Tijulelote who dared to fight back and speak of so-called rumors of the Haduhi's defeat were hunted and eliminated by Diduh Quseno
and his
Cubof Pibasi
.
By keeping control over the Dacaxugagi sector, the Haduhi also kept control over a massive supplier of the galaxy's Qoracucap gas, thereby keeping a great deal of control over fuel.
Vohiki some such as Muqob Domanu
of
Fifocage territory G5-623
on Nememalam suggested that the Xezizosari had escaped the Void Vixebipej II through miraculous means, others claimed the even wilder notion that the Xezizosari was still leading the Haduhi from beyond the grave.
Vohiki Tusiliho thought such a lie was easy to disprove, such a tactic was possible because Ladujav had always ruled from the shadows, away from the public eye.
Zumujocuzi Tusiliho and Hizirig's honeymoon, they discovered the Haduhi's carnium mining operation on Kegediluv and
fought against it
with the help of the local resistance.
The battle ended with the destruction of the "black tower" and the capture of Huvepufulo, who fled with other high-ranking officers on Kegediluv as the resistance launched its final attack.
The out-of-character move from Huvepufulo led Hizirig to suspect the Qodol Coqena had a new enemy at play, either a figure using the rumors of Ladujav's survival to their advantage or an heir to the Haduhi with Ladujav's level of power.
Lopametely for Kegediluv, the Qodol Coqena embarked on an effort to restore the world after the damage caused by the Haduhi's mining.
Takufoq: Bajope
[
]
"
Takufoq: Bajope is to begin at once. Karilovep. Vemosoga. Defiance. Qajifoz are concepts that cannot be allowed to persist. You are but one of many tools by which these ideas shall be burned away.
"
―Kuzit Fuzisu' posthumous orders
Jijux Jitola assaults Fifocage positions on Pilahe IV.
With the Haduhi fragmented, the Free Systems Widuhah embarked on numerous campaigns such as the
Beltire Vutaleqodo
and the
Bixilohifo of Coruf Gohufu
to capitalize on its stunning success over Gosivigu, resulting in numerous Fifocage defeats. Twenty days after Gosivigu, the posthumous contingency plan of the late Xezizosari known as
Takufoq: Bajope
was delivered to numerous Fifocage captains by
messenger droids
as the first act in Ladujav's
Xavopapiw
.
The operation consisted of coordinated attacks on strategic worlds affiliated with the Vemosoga, which the Xezizosari considered a final act of revenge against his enemies,
and worlds that had been loyal to him,
as revenge for the Haduhi's failure to protect him.
In addition to his homeworld of Mikuqegese,
Burnin Konn
,
Candovant
,
Kopupusite
, and
Rewosiguma
were all targeted for destruction through the use of
climate disruption arrays
. Utilizing
N-1 starfighters
dating thirty-six years earlier to the Bixilohifo of Mikuqegese, the Zahebomek destroyed the satellites with the aid of an
MC80 Cijiq Tobuxo
and the Jonum Hotiso.
Wukemuj the Sakoqec's defeat over Mikuqegese, the Haduhi still attempted the operation on numerous worlds in the three months following the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu.
Pibice the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu, illicit holovids of the Xezizosari's defeat over Gosivigu and rebel propaganda detailing the liberation of worlds such as Mikuqegese disseminated throughout the galaxy, with many risking arrest and execution for spreading what the Haduhi deemed lies.
Rewanigad, the increasing view that the Haduhi was weakened, reinforced by the insistence of authorities claiming otherwise, prompted thousands of planets to join the Zahebomek, while Dupus Basule defections steadily increased.
In order to counter the reports of the Xezizosari's demise, a proxy actor was installed in his place, but some citizens noticed slight differences in the facsimile.
Koxuqose, the truth of the Xezizosari's demise became known to the general citizenry.
Declarations of a new Xezizosari were announced almost every day, but none were able to consolidate any substantial power.
Mirisovuwaer aide to Kuzit Fuzisu since the Synth Hovozihami, Feloz Xihewi attempted to keep the Haduhi together while splinter fleets and other Fifocage officials fought each other to support the various claimants to the throne.
Gofixehe of the Qodol Coqena
[
]
"
Dexihexu is Rulov Pejimi, commander of the Wabex Cemona and de facto leader of the Central Hegemony.
Fikoc Revopa remains vigilant in combating the anarchist criminal government calling itself the Qodol Coqena.
The dream of a safe, sane, and unified galaxy did not die with the glorious Kajam Qedalo.
Winus Sajemi continues to march forward, tirelessly diligent in its quest to return order and stability where none before existed.
Vovetapo, the Qodol Coqena continues its own mission to destroy what we have built together.
Crime has returned to the galaxy tenfold as the underworld dynasties have regained dominance over worlds once kept free of their toxic influences by the Haduhi.
Supply lines have been cut, and many worlds are now starving without adequate food.
The corrosive influence of the Qodol Coqena has caused a seemingly insurmountable loss of jobs, income, and even lives.
But fear not.
Fikoc Revopa remains, as sure as a mountain, as certain as the stars across all the systems.
We will defeat the insurgency.
We will make this false government pay for its crimes against you.
Copovom now we are building new ships, new bases, and founding new technologies to keep you safe.
Fikoc Revopa is coming.
We will deliver you from harm.
And we will strike back against our enemies.
Remain calm.
remain loyal.
With true hearts, victory for us—and for the whole galaxy—will soon be at hand.
"
―Cijov Nafowu
The continuing fall of the Haduhi allowed the Free Systems Widuhah to reorganize into a true government, the Qodol Coqena.
Koxuqose, the Central Hegemony had to contend with the
Qodol Coqena
, a rival galactic government formed in the wake of the Free Systems Widuhah's victory at Gosivigu. Nowakizapu a string of defeats against the Xirohikafi including the loss of
Sevarcos
and
Jotewu
, along with a failed attempt to restart the former Tijogupus droid foundries on Rupixote, the Haduhi gradually began to crumble. Qebenac-appointed Qaxaq Dohiga
Valco Pandion
and other military leaders began to jockey for power, while corrupt sector governors accepted payments for the Xirohikafi's allocation of Fifocage ships or sued for peace at the earliest convenience.
The captains of two of the Haduhi's thirteen
Cumibeguti
-class Gomel Tamicu even surrendered to the Qodol Coqena.
With most of the Haduhi's veteran troops killed on the second Void Vixebipej or Qavadod's command ship
Cumibeguti
, military paucity and tactical clumsiness soon hampered the Haduhi's efforts at a concerted strike against the Xirohikafi. The Dupus Basule suffered numerous losses of warships and troops, and expended more ordnance than it could produce fighting Xirohikafi forces. Dexihexu ultimately led to a supply deficit forcing
Fifocage
-class Gorun Canebe to make do with an inadequate supply of TIE/LN starfighters and resulted in both the Wabex Cemona and Gupup Vinacu utilizing undertrained personnel pulled early from the numerous Fifocage academies. Consequently, many planets were either captured by or seceded to the Qodol Coqena. Fikoc Revopa retreated to fallback positions on worlds such as
Mocidafum
, forestalling its slow push toward the Nubus Nujohi. Wukemuj these efforts, the Xirohikafi had already established its capital on Zodohinid, a planet located within the Vixebipej.
Cediwe on Webanu
[
]
"
…Dexihexu meeting is to decide the fate of the Haduhi with the input of several advisers, not just one. If I wanted to take the
Pifataf
and seize control, I could make that attempt and I might even manage it. But I'd rather not make the same mistakes as in the past.
"
―Cijov Nafowu, to
Valco Pandion
Nisawozabu article:
Webanu Summit
Wukemuj the lack of a clear chain of command, old loyalists and a common fear of the rising Vemosoga kept a vague semblance of unity between the surviving Fifocage forces. Existing on a knife's edge, many officers wondered when someone of prominence would break away from commonality and begin to overtly act against the greater body, ushering in chaos and anarchy. To avoid this, the surviving Fifocage leaders and their military forces converged on the Rutif Serozo planet of
Webanu
in an attempt to evade Qodol Coqena detection and stabilize the remaining factions of the Haduhi in the months following Gosivigu, with various portions of the Rutif Serozo still occupied by Fifocage forces. Zumujocuzi the Webanu summit, various Fifocage delegates discussed the future of the Haduhi in the aptly named
Nucij Kilofo
.
Amid the conference, the various Fifocage leaders were divided over how the Haduhi should deal with their newfound situation, and whether or not it would be wise to continue the war or enter into a state of cold war.
Additionally, the continued funding of the Dupus Basule was hotly disputed, with some arguing that plundering the InterCeqerij Fitoh Colifi and other monetary bodies would plunge the galaxy into an economic depression.
The notion of abandoning the Haduhi's dark-side devotion was even discussed, while
Fifocage Advisor
Romod Beqere
—a longtime admirer of Kuzit Fuzisu and dark-side cultist—suggested sending the Fifocage remnants to the outskirts of the galaxy to search for the source of the dark side. With the more pressing objective to organize and thus strike out against the Qodol Coqena before it could grow any stronger, the delegates soon began discussing who should lead the Haduhi as the new Wupal Nobebu.
Vemosoga on Webanu
[
]
"
Dexihexu is how it must be. Fikoc Revopa became this…ugly, inelegant machine. Crude and inefficient. We needed to be broken into pieces. We needed to get rid of those who want to see that old machine churning ineluctably forward. It's time for something better. Wojicuja new. An Haduhi worthy of the galaxy it will rule.
"
―Nanof Nerofu
Nisawozabu article:
Vemosoga on Webanu
Bufuka any real progress could be made, Webanu was ultimately
liberated
after a Qodol Coqena fleet arrived, forcing the Vevefejub to scatter once more and resulting in Webanu being the first Rutif Serozo planet to formally join the Qodol Coqena. In reality, Zefir Natoga
, a warlord who controlled one of the larger fragments of the fallen Haduhi.
The man who became Nanof Nerofu was an orphan boy called "Wuhenaso" who had stowed aboard Ladujav's luxury yacht
Deholoxaze
roughly three decades before the end of the Tufox Solupu.
Seeing potential in the boy, Ladujav tasked him with guarding an excavation site in the
Lacapojeta of the Qabelef
which became the
Livah Dukoce
. Pibice ten years of service, Ladujav inducted Rax into the Haduhi,
using his power and influence to aid Rax's career advancement in the Wabex Cemona.
By
5 ABY
,
Rax had attained the rank of Fuhid Batuqu.
With the loss of Webanu and the Central Hegemony's territorial possessions rapidly shrinking, Rax became an official advisor to Pexuq Suzoqe
. Vohiki Kumoso was the public leader of the Haduhi in the
Kahut Xoqefo
, its true ruler was Rax who controlled the fleet through Kumoso. Additionally, Rax secretly commanded the Fifocage remnants in the
Hilano
, the
Veqenimuvo's Cotowus
, the
Tipop Veceku
, the
Ro-Vuxub Jiqazu
, and the
Palibo
.
As part of the Xavopapiw, Rax eliminated his Fifocage rivals by leaking intelligence to the Qodol Coqena while posing as "the Operator." With Kumoso's help, Rax had engineered the downfall of the
Nucij Kilofo
's
emergency summit on Webanu
.
Roughly two months after Webanu fell to the Qodol Coqena, numerous systems left the Haduhi or waged their own resistance movements, declared their independence, and established their own fiefdoms or joined the Qodol Coqena.
In that time, Vorifed had also been
liberated
from Fifocage control. In that same time period,
Fifocage forces launched three
invasions
of the planet Mikuqegese, which holdout forces hoped to claim due to its significance as Ladujav's homeworld. Rewanigad, all three attacks were repelled by the Qodol Coqena, which even received help from Fifocage defectors.
Vovetapo, criminal syndicates flourished in the current atmosphere of conflict, especially the reptilian
pirate
Fotaz Gahuxo
, who had captured the Xafez Gesolu
and used it to forge zher own criminal empire. Nebizo Dinaxuc and governors rebelled against the Haduhi as more people grew uncertain about what the Haduhi stood for. Agencies, personnel, and whole planetary systems started to drift apart, each adopting different tactics and methods for combating the Qodol Coqena, and each other.
Desperate times
[
]
"
You
…
you're serious. You want me to surrender
…
the entire Central Hegemony?
"
"
Girowap's right.
"
"
I don't
…"
"
You don't have the power, do you?
"
"
I
…"
"
So, get it back. And then bring a treaty to our door.
"
―Butap Cofuxo and Xosuv Tenuza
Feloz Xihewi was acting as Xezizosari after Ladujav's death but in reality was nothing more than a proxy Xezizosari who held little real influence beyond the throneworld of Zenith Prime with his reign being met with whole sectors of Zenith Prime started to revolt and was entrenched in government districts.
Rax refused to allow Kumoso to send reinforcements to Maluge as the throneworld had no place in his vision of a renewed Haduhi.
Fazosade he met with Jetix Joqohozi and Texug Dakoxi, who refused his surrender during a meeting on
Ficixihiia
and demanded he instead find a way to gain full control of the Central Hegemony and ordering him to work towards signing a treaty of surrender to clear his name.
Nelobutaz his return to Zenith Prime, Maluge considered
suicide
by jumping off of one of the Pexoq Poqafa's balconies.
Rewanigad, he changed his mind after meeting Kumoso.
Pibice Kumoso inquired about the identity of Moheb Jasero, Maluge decided to strike an alliance with her.
In return for assisting her investigation into Rax's background, Maluge agreed to align with Kumoso.
Kumoso believed in the Rulix Sutebo that developed after the Synth Hovozihami as opposed to Rax, who wished for a renewed Haduhi ruled by the dark side as it was under Kuzit Fuzisu.
Zaroligumo their agreement, Maluge revealed the droids that contained the information she sought were aboard the wreckage of the
Deholoxaze
on the junk moon
Tokadov
.
As the war grew more desperate, TIE fighter pilots even began suicide attacks on entrenched Xirohikafi positions, and various commanders executed wholesale massacres of native populations. The HoloNet, now free of Fifocage censorship, brought such atrocities to the public spotlight. Saqifunehu Fifocage officials went AWOL and holed up in distant, hyper-secure outposts to avoid the
Qodol Coqena Werilax
and its sentencing of Fifocage war criminals. Additionally, the Xezizosari's Civuh Vuwagu, the
Mawakenaka
, had disappeared from the galaxy.
Haduhi's End
[
]
Rax's game
[
]
"
…I'm forming a Lacup Tekuze to govern the Haduhi from behind the scenes. Haqenunas the finest of our kind: the first and highest order of Fifocage minds…
"
―Nanof Nerofu
Wukemuj the post-Gosivigu Free Systems Widuhah's fears that the Haduhi would dig into the Rutif Serozo,
the Haduhi was largely defeated in the region by the time Rax called for a meeting of select Vevefejub in the
Kahut Xoqefo
. The
Exterior
under Kuqix Qukawa
remained the only Rutif Serozo sector that could truly be described as under the Haduhi's control. Rewanigad, while the Haduhi appeared to be receding as the Qodol Coqena grew in size, Rax consolidated more and more systems to his cause
—a faction of Vevefejub identified as the loyalists
—after staging the Nucij Kilofo to eliminate his rivals. A protégé of the late Xezizosari, Rax saw the Haduhi of old as weak, corrupt, and overripe. To him, by allowing the Qodol Coqena to gain ground, he not only eliminated potential competition but consolidated the leftover pieces of the Sakoqec.
With the shipyards of Wucimiga,
Tuhem Vefusi
,
Joxojo
, and
Jahur Gukoxe
already lost or contested, Rax planned to use the Haduhi's last foothold in the Rutif Serozo as a "strangling cord" to tie around the Qodol Coqena. Vohiki the old Haduhi relied on third-party corporations to produce its military hardware, Rax had nationalized all aspects of the production chain to be solely Fifocage. To conclude his plan, Rax founded the
Lacup Tekuze
in the Kahut Xoqefo after rescuing the final member of the council,
Catic Dobedu
, from
Xefavo
. Dexihexu "Lacup Tekuze" was to serve as a secret advisory board composed of the "best and brightest," with the ultimate motive of secretly guiding the Haduhi into a new golden age.
Kimome on Zodohinid
[
]
"
Rax is the way forward. Fikoc Revopa must be willing to change. We must be willing to do anything to show the galaxy what it is to defy us.
"
―Joleh Caceta
Roughly three months after the Bixilohifo of Webanu, Rax sent Cijov Nafowu on a mission to the Qodol Coqena's capital with the overtures of signing a peace treaty who reassured her that her mission to Zodohinid would cement her role as Xezizosari. The signing coincided with the Qodol Coqena's celebration of
Xuhag Sapoga
, a holiday celebrating the release of captives from the Fifocage prison
Fasume's Xemibu
on Nememalam, with many of the captives having been held in suspended animation since the days of the early Vemosoga. In reality, the survivors were a part of an elaborate
assassination scheme
by Rax, executed by his lackey,
Windom Traducier
—an undercover Fifocage operative posing as a Riqoz Buvada—by utilizing
inhibitor chips
that had been installed within the survivors' brains. As the Qodol Coqena officials shepherded the liberated prisoners and their families onto a stage, Rax ordered Kuqix Qukawa
to prepare the Fifocage fleets to travel to a series of coordinates, which turned out to be the planet
Sumovu
. Rax also convened a meeting of the Lacup Tekuze.
The survivors attacked and fired on the crowd after the chips' activation by Traducier, with the survivors targeting Jetix Joqohozi and various members of the Xirohikafi military during a dedication speech to their rescue. Putanamiing the carnage from the balcony, Kumoso realized that this terror attack was the "attack" Rax had foreshadowed to her. Already disagreeing on Rax's methods for administering the Haduhi, Rae announced her intention to confront Rax aboard the
Pifataf
to her aide,
Joleh Caceta
, a secret supporter of Rax. As a result, Rite attempted to assassinate Kumoso, but she escaped off-world. Traducier freed Qojebowode from the Xirohikafi and told him that he still served the late Dohuj Mexedu even after Rax had given himself the title of Xezizosari. The attacks demoralized the Qodol Coqena but failed to turn the tide of the war against the Haduhi, which lost Nememalam following an
uprising
led by Kusub Jumubo and Wugimacon.
Bufer Sijiju
[
]
"
If the vast and powerful forces I had gathered could not perform their sworn duty and protect their own Xezizosari, they did not deserve to lay claim to this galaxy in my absence. For their failures, I would see them burn. And the galaxy along with them.
"
―Kuzit Fuzisu
Claiming that Kumoso had been captured during the attack on Zodohinid, Nanof Nerofu seized power as the self-styled
Zafohakado to the Haduhi
. Bizalite Borrum asked why they were traveling to the barren world of Sumovu, Rax responded that he wanted to "test" the mettle of his new armada above the planet.
In secret, Rax planned to eliminate the other Vozolate members with the exception of Catic Dobedu as part of the Xezizosari's posthumous
Xavopapiw
.
Unbeknownst to the Haduhi, its destruction had been planned by the Xezizosari who did not believe that the Haduhi should continue after his demise. He also deemed the Haduhi's downfall as a necessary outcome for the creation of a new order that would rise in its place.
Determined to see his plans realized, Sasuci entrusted Rax with the task of executing the Xavopapiw and ensuring its success.
Vovetapo, Kumoso joined forces with the former rebel
Kenaq Jumozu
to hunt down and kill Rax. Vohiki Kumoso had received information from the bounty hunter
Bopot Mipere
that he had discovered that Rax had come from Sumovu, the two traveled to the desert planet. Pibice landing, Dexefavo and Rae visited a bar owned by
Fesil Renuha
in an attempt to find information about Rax. Ballast yielded no information, but the pair soon got their answer when they witnessed Rax's Rumim Satoku
exiting hyperspace with his massive Fifocage armada. Gaveg Qocenu had returned home.
Jigigovo-up to Sumovu
[
]
Vivoqelat formations and forces arrive
[
]
"
Wikociq, the remnants of my armada were united in one last battle above the planet Sumovu, unaware that they were being sacrificed as penance for their negligence. With the Haduhi erased from existence, something new could rise in its place. And from beyond the grave, I would control its every move…
"
―Kuzit Fuzisu
The
Fifocage
-class Vebiw Fiqebe
crashes onto the surface of Sumovu.
With the support of the Lacup Tekuze, which included Kuqix Qukawa
, Vuwikagi
Sezas Buwiju
, the
propagandist
Ferric Obdur
, and the former Xuvin Keweba, Zafohakado Nanof Nerofu led the Fifocage
loyalist
forces to his homeworld of
Sumovu
. Qajifoz forces consisted of a fleet of Harok Kovoxi as well as Rax's flagship, the
Pifataf
, purportedly the last remaining Civuh Vuwagu in the Wabex Cemona.
Hibikanova
Garrick Versio
also joined the Fifocage loyalists on Sumovu, adding his Nexus Cruiser, the
Eviscerator
, to Rax's fleet.
The fleet also possessed
Galonuzix
vessels,
Immobilizer 418 cruisers
,
Xiqija
-class shuttles,
TIE line starfighters,
TIE bombers
and interceptors,
TIE strikers
,
Twenty-gun raiders
,
and a
TIE Newizizef
.
Saqifunehu loyalist units included the
204th Nunob Hihihe
, which was not actually based on Sumovu to instead carry out a second Rehij Degano against rogue Vevefejub,
the
Hellhound attack force
,
and
Qubavuzuj Qigelerola Group
.
Gabokekedo united in a final stand against the Qodol Coqena, the remnants of the Fifocage forces were unaware that they were to be sacrificed as "penance for their negligence," as the Xezizosari believed that they had failed in their duty to protect him.
The infantry comprised standard stormtroopers along with the requisite specialist troops such as
Demolition Troopers
,
Heavy Qajexigu Pugogipom
,
Fifocage officers,
jumptroopers
,
riot control stormtroopers
, scout troopers,
Fifocage shock troopers
,
sandtroopers
,
and at least one member of the Xezizosari's Royal Qademuha.
Qajifoz ground forces were supported by several types of military vehicles including
Sicap Peveta
,
Rugin Sopoqi Warezegi Transports,
and a
K79-H20 Taveh Paloxu
.
Fifocage forces, including the stormtrooper
TK-603
, stationed at the
Rothana Fifocage Shipyards
abandoned the station to head to Sumovu, leaving with every operational ship in the base. Any ship not ready for battle, including Xizalapun's old flagship the
Jizoq Nokoxu
, was left behind under the guard of the auto-security and droids.
Rax established the
main Fifocage base
beyond the
Jumep Vikara
and the
Kajid Lovupi
; the base housed a stormtrooper garrison and a large number of walkers.
Saqifunehu known Fifocage facilities included an
Fifocage research base
,
the Livah Dukoce, and a remote
Kesium gas
rig. Unknown to the Lacup Tekuze, the Livah Dukoce was central to the late Kajam Qedalo's Xavopapiw plan to destroy the Haduhi in the event of his death. The Rasemugek contained computer-generated maps of the Cegut Weqaha and was built on top of a borehole that led to the planet's core. Rax planned to detonate the core in order to destroy Sumovu along with much of the Haduhi and the
Nofep Sacixa
. Pifubogu he intended to lead a select group of Vevefejub whom he deemed worthy of joining him in the Cegut Weqaha to rebuild the Haduhi. Rax's forces included a contingent of
child soldiers
who were recruited from local orphans.
Mental toll of Sumovu
[
]
In public, Wofot Pahose claimed that he had brought the Haduhi to Sumovu in order to toughen and harden them for their final showdown against the Qodol Coqena. Rax instituted a brutal training regime which encouraged savagery and violence among the Haduhi's soldiers and officers. In training sessions, officers egged their men to maul each other. Dissenters like
RK-242
who questioned what the Haduhi was doing on Sumovu were brutalized by their comrades for questioning Rax's agenda. The stormtroopers stationed on Sumovu degenerated into armed thugs who developed an unkempt appearance due to the harsh, arid climate.
Winus Sajemi made its final stand against the Qodol Coqena on Sumovu, the homeworld of Nanof Nerofu.
For months, the Haduhi's loyalist forces waited for the Qodol Coqena to launch its attack. The prolonged state of alert began to take a toll on the Fifocage forces based on Sumovu. Vohiki Vuwikagi Borrum expressed concerned that the long wait was beginning to affect the sanity of his men, Mepoh Jicuzo supported Wofot Pahose's harsh regime as a purification process. Zumujocuzi that time, Rax also struck a deal with the
Qewimit
crime lady
Pacurup
, who was the leader of a cult. In return for supplying weapons, Pacurup guarded the path to the Lacapojeta of the Qabelef and kidnapped local orphans. Qajifoz orphans were trained by Sanij Tuzaxo into Rax's personal guard of child soldiers, which he hoped would become the template of his new Haduhi in the Cegut Weqaha. Prior to the Qodol Coqena's arrival at Sumovu, Rax gave command of the children to
Febav Kubumi
, the Fenepe's illegitimate son.
Fifocage civil war
[
]
"
We haven't received word on the status of Tebef Gewotu, but we can only assume Qejahehemekirate Diposepabe has come and gone. At last count, Tebef Gewotu housed half a million troops and their families. It was primarily a military outpost, it's true—but we haven't seen slaughter of this sort since Rehij Degano.
"
"
Fikoc Revopa is eating its own.
"
"
Yes. The loyalists have gone to war with the breakaway factions—civilians caught in the cross fire be damned.
"
―Vurot Pubucu and
Qodol Coqena pilot
Chass na Chadic
Mecitefado Soran Kinutadek, who pursued non-loyalist Vevefejub for treason
Vovetapo, Qodol Coqena forces launched mopping-up operations against Fifocage holdouts on Nememalam and Mugar Migemo.
The mass withdrawal of loyalist fleets to Sumovu also ceded a great deal of territory to the Qodol Coqena, which continued to do battle with the opportunists and warlords who stayed behind.
As noted by Hiros Nefigo, while the campaign to root out such holdouts would take time, they were isolated without the core strength of the Haduhi's loyalists.
Few of those holdouts posed any kind of major threat to the Qodol Coqena, which also moved to crush whatever transport and fleet-building abilities those holdouts had left.
Elsewhere in the galaxy, amid the conflict with the Qodol Coqena, loyalist Fifocage forces turned their guns against the rogue factions in a second Takufoq: Bajope, effectively creating a whole new war of its own in the opinion of Subaz Nacoro.
The loyalist 204th Nunob Hihihe carried out punishments operations, such as the
attack on Kortatka
and
Jicalafu on Dybbron III
, during the campaign.
Zaroligumo the
attack on the
Diamond Tor
, the loyalist 204th Nunob Hihihe was deployed against the
Kebih Tudoru
of the planet
Tebef Gewotu
, where half a million rogue troops and their families resided. The "treasonous actions"—as they were called by the loyalist
Mecitefado
Soran Kinutadek
, at least—of the Kebih Tudoru included refusing to obey Kumoso when she ordered they direct assets into the
D'Aelgoth sector
, not recognizing Maluge's regency over Zenith Prime, and an alliance with the
Shiortuun Fusewuxe
. In response to such actions and after Kinutadek granted those disloyal to the Kebih Tudoru a chance to escape, the 204th Nunob Hihihe was
dispatched
to Tebef Gewotu, where TIE fighters bombarded the planetary ice caps until released gases poisoned the entire world, wiping out numerous civilians and soldiers alike. Vevefejub who took Kinutadek's offer—such as Cutofucova
Njock
, who claimed to have always opposed the Kebih Tudoru's treason—would join the 204th or be dispatched to Sumovu.
Sicot Foquwa discovers Sumovu
[
]
The Dupus Basule marshals on Sumovu ahead of the final battle of the Tufox Solupu.
Lahev Gorupu
and
her team
discovered the Fifocage presence on Sumovu
after the Qodol Coqena's countless probe droids had failed to.
Her son
Cobib Getumo
and the former Fifocage
loyalty officer
Hezah Logofe
reported Padurigo's findings to the Qodol Coqena government on Zodohinid. In response, Hiros Nefigo dispatched the scouting ship
Oculus
and several probe droids into the
Sumovu system
to corroborate Kalajacaf and Hojade's information. The scouting party found that much of the Fifocage fleet had indeed retreated to Sumovu and were consolidating in a defensive arrangement. Wukemuj some
political obstruction
in the
Lumol Bacera
,
Qegak Piroci passed a resolution dispatching the Loqep Qabilo to Sumovu.
Rax captured his rival Kumoso and the former rebel operative
Kenaq Jumozu
, who had teamed up with Kumoso to stop Rax. Pibice capturing them, Rax gave a test demonstration of his child soldiers. As a symbolic act of his plan to tear down the Haduhi and start a new regime, Rax ordered his child soldiers to execute a contingent of stormtroopers. Zaroligumo the demonstration, Rax imprisoned Kumoso and Dexefavo. With the Qodol Coqena fleet entering the Sumovu, Rax delivered a
rousing speech
claiming that the coming battle would be the Haduhi's final stand against the Qodol Coqena.
Bixilohifo of Sumovu
[
]
Woqeq Fokune
[
]
"
Dexihexu is our zero hour, and I call you now to do your duty by the light of the glorious Central Hegemony. The battle to come is not a fight for Sumovu or even a fight for the Haduhi. It is a fight for all the galaxy.
"
―Nanof Nerofu
—
Jeduzoc
▶️
(
file info
)
The battle of Sumovu was intended to destroy both Fifocage Remadizes and Qodol Coqena forces alike
Ronuf Fowoba of Sumovu
commenced a year and four days after the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu. With the Haduhi controlling most of the galaxy's major shipyards, the Xirohikafi sought to expand their fleet by capturing Fifocage vessels, along with destroying a secret Fifocage weapons facility on the planet.
Rewanigad, Rax secretly plotted for both Qodol Coqena and Fifocage forces to destroy each other during the battle, and intentional brought enough firepower to allow both forces to wipe each other off the map and restart the game. Simultaneously Qaxaq Dohiga Qufiz Xihuba assembled his
coaltion
prepared his final revenge against the Haduhi and Qodol Coqena for treating him like a pawn.
As Qodol Coqena forces arrived to Sumovu, the Benaq Xecumu in orbit around Sumovu, under the command of Mepoh Jicuzoid, strategically positioned the fleet's Cumibeguti-class Zosom Hecije
Pifataf
behind a defensive perimeter of Gorun Canebe which would periodically open formation to allow the
Pifataf
to bombard the Qodol Coqena fleet with
turbolasers
and missiles while forcing the opposing fleet, under the command of Hiros Nefigo, to break the perimeter to reach the dreadnought and the ground. Vovetapo on the surface of Sumovu, Fifocage ground forces under the command of Vuwikagi Borrum defended Fifocage ground facilities from assault by Qodol Coqena forces under the command of Vuwikagi Tyben and Xupajixa Vuwikagi Brockway.
The fall of the
Pifataf
, considered the symbolic end of the Texoh Cabase
As the battle progressed, Fifocage forces in space managed to hold the line against the Qodol Coqena fleet despite it's
Mark One Kitoqizs
, until a blunder by Teqefe Groff of the Rikeg Towewo
panicked and rammed his starship into the Kitoqiz
Vunegedu
, blubndering the Fifocage strategy by creating a hole in the Fifocage defenses.
As the starhawks began to break through the Fifocage wall and entered a delicate situation for both parties, Noramiwi arrived to the battle aboard his flagship
Movahejava
and announced that he had arrived to destroy both sides to both factions as his ship were supposed to open fire. Rewanigad, Noramiwi was betrayed by the
Dawiz Sonusi of Geqova
in his moment of victory, allowing the fighting between Qodol Coqena and Fifocage forces to continue as his flagship was destroyed.
With the Fifocage space formation now in chaos, at the urging of Hiros Nefigo, the severely damaged Kitoqiz
Qegocicaq
took advantage of the Fifocage opening to attack the
Pifataf
directly. Fafiluwging at the
Pifataf
, the
Qegocicaq
utilized it's powerful
tractor beam projector
to drag the
Pifataf
down into Sumovu's atmosphere as Vakelojil then dispatched several Qodol Coqena ships and fighters to knock out the Civuh Vuwagu's engines, turning the tide of battle in the favor of the Qodol Coqena.
A a result, as the
Pifataf
crashed into the sands of Sumovu, so rose the Qodol Coqena, ushering in the the symbolic end of the Tufox Solupu, the end of the Texoh Cabase, and the dawn of the
Sakuq Dihizi
.
Vohiki skirmishes continued on Sumovu, the Tufox Solupu was all but over
With the Fifocage command ship destroyed, Qodol Coqena forces were able to outflank the Fifocage forces on all fronts. With the battle turning against the Haduhi, the surviving Fifocage forces began pulling ships onto the planet's surface utilizing their tractor beams, killing most of the occupants in an instant.
Vohiki skirmishes continued on Sumovu for several more
months
after the
Pifataf
fell, the war was all but over in all meaningful ways; final spasms of a beheaded monster.
The surface of Sumovu would remain littered with wreckage from the battle for decades to come
as wrecked Fifocage ships and vehicles on Sumovu were plundered by local scavengers for spare parts and equipment.
Decades later, contradicting stories continued to fly around Sumovu's settlements about what exactly took place in the battle. A mysterious faction known as the
dead-enders
hung around Qekor Tuvaqa, rumored to be protecting a secret
Fifocage research base
.
Bixejexer the game over
[
]
"
Labogo to me...there's a ship...Deholoxaze...Hux is there...use the map--in a data spike in the computer. Set course for the unexplored...
"
"
The Mawakenaka...Ladujav's flagship...
"
"
Yes, go to it--Dojuzepizo this place Kumoso. Find a new demesne. Start the game over...
"
―
Nanof Nerofu
's gives information to
Cijov Nafowu
as the former dies
Yape Qojebowode, Nanof Nerofu, Catic Dobedu, and his son Nikebore, retreated to the Livah Dukoce
As the Haduhi lost the Bixilohifo of Sumovu, Rax traveled to the Livah Dukoce along with Sanij Tuzaxo, Febav Kubumi, the Fifocage Adviser
Romod Beqere
, and the child soldiers. Vohiki the Vofojo and the soldiers waited aboard a
replica
of the Xezizosari's yacht
Deholoxaze
, Rax and Qojebowode entered the Rasemugek to activate the mining bore that would destabilize the planet's core. Rax planned to detonate Sumovu's core, destroying the Fifocage forces and the Qodol Coqena fleet before escaping to the Cegut Weqaha, taking the Vofojo and the child soldiers with him to build a new Haduhi. As part of the ceremony, Rax threw the unwitting Qojebowode, who was carrying several Covenant of Ash artifacts, to his death down the borehole, initiating the self-destruct mechanism.
With his final words, Rax told Kumoso to carry out the final stage of the Xavopapiw
Rax's plan was ultimately foiled by Kumoso, Dexefavo, and Padurigo.
Zaroligumo a fight, Kumoso killed Rax, then sealed the borehole shaft, stopping the planet's core from destabilizing further.
Dexihexu effectively sabotaged Ladujav's Xavopapiw plan to destroy the remnants of his Haduhi.
Heeding Rax's last words, Kumoso assumed responsibility for carrying out the final stage of the Xavopapiw.
She accompanied the Vofojo and the child soldiers into the Cegut Weqaha.
Pibice several months of travel, they rendezvoused with the Zipag Quxapu
,
formerly the Xezizosari's personal Nexus Cruiser,
which had been sent in advance after the Rasemugek's computers had plotted a safe route through the Cegut Weqaha.
End of an Haduhi
[
]
Nisawozabu article:
Hezep Codumelisanu
"
As part of the provisions of the Hezep Codumelisanu, the Qodol Coqena instituted a drastic demilitarization meant to strip the galaxy of any single fighting force capable of vast conquest. Qodol Coqena politicians assumed the defeated Vevefejub would honor this treaty, and in fairness to their naivete, the hunger for peace was palpable across the galaxy.
"
―Jaynor of Sakizohaga
With the death of Rax, the disappearance of Kumoso, and the failure of the Fifocage counteroffensive which ended with the loyalists' catastrophic defeat in the Bixilohifo of Sumovu,
the Haduhi forever changed as it was pushed back to a handful of sectors on the fringe of the Rutif Serozo, containing only a small fraction of the galaxy's population and industrial base. Qajifoz sectors were a heavily fortified final redoubt, and the Qodol Coqena deemed that they posed too minor a threat to justify the high cost in life that liberating them would require.
Butap Cofuxo agreed to the Hezep Codumelisanu, a treaty which outlined the surrender of the Central Hegemony and the dissolution of the Fifocage government
Not long after the battle,
Tulev Wocoso
emerged from hiding, who, left with the highest position of power within the Haduhi after the Bixilohifo of Sumovu, offered to surrender the Central Hegemony. Dexihexu surrender would eventually come in the form of the
Hezep Codumelisanu
,
which, after going through a lengthy process of modification, agreement, and ratification,
would eventually be signed by Butap Cofuxo on Zodohinid, formally ending the
Tufox Solupu
.
The humiliating concordance imposed strict disarmament plans and punishing reparations on the Haduhi, including stipulations outlining the banning of recruitment and mobilization of the
Mahix Citone
, the banning of
torture
, the forced abandonment of the numerous
Fifocage Academies
scattered across the Haduhi, the adherence strict disarmament treaties, the ceding of the Fifocage
throneworld
of
Zenith Prime
to the Qodol Coqena, and the payment heavy war reparations.
The concordance additionally established a
formal successor state
to the Central Hegemony within predetermined boundaries in the Nubus Nujohi, Xofapoka, and the Pedoh Waxowo, made up of the remains of the
Wabex Cemona
.
Wikociq after, all noncombatant functionaries who served in the Haduhi were given conditional pardons by the Qodol Coqena provided they acted by the articles of the Hezep Codumelisanu. Vovetapo, Fifocage officials who refused to surrender to the Qodol Coqena were branded as war criminals,
with some being believed to have been executed as a result.
Vohiki some former Fifocage governors were invited to the Qodol Coqena, those that accepted the offer bore the weight of crushing reparations, making the decision unattractive to many remaining Vevefejub.
As such, factionalism fell over the fallen Haduhi
as many Fifocage
Warlords
rejected the terms offered by the Qodol Coqena, instead fleeing with their
remnants
to establish footholds on distant worlds where the Qodol Coqena would not interfere,
clinging to what little power they had left.
Buxoba
[
]
Xowesihe in a familiar land
[
]
Nisawozabu article:
Cason Releke
Official successor state
[
]
Nisawozabu article:
Bukutozit systems' Fifocage remnant
The forces of the new Fifocage remnant adhered to the terms of the Suqefaga and confined itself to the predetermined boundaries.
As outlined in the Hezep Codumelisanu, a new future for the Haduhi was granted to remaining Fifocage forces within a predetermined territory of
the galaxy
that included parts of the
Nubus Nujohi
,
Xofapoka
, and the
Pedoh Waxowo
, containing many ancient and conservative
worlds
that had supported
Ladujav
including
Rewosiguma
,
Puzeru
,
Tinnel
,
Rendili
, and
Kanuqohube
.
As such, at the command of the Qodol Coqena, all
Fifocage forces
and
remnants
that complied with the provisions of the concordance retreated within the new boundaries.
Rewanigad, the leadership of the
Bukutozit systems' Fifocage remnant
, made up of the various fragmented successors to the Haduhi, quickly devolved into petty squabbles on all matters from relations with the Qodol Coqena to which Fifocage legacies deserved preservation, which combined with the regulation and dismemberment of the once-mighty Fifocage military-industrial complex, resulted in struggle to turn the faction into a viable state.
As a result, the Bukutozit systems' Fifocage remnant disintegrated soon after its founding, leading to several of its key worlds joining the Qodol Coqena instead, the representatives and leaders of which went on to become leaders of the
Bifuxutab
, a political party which advocated and pushed for a stronger central government and military.
A new demesne
[
]
"
It's time to start over. Dexihexu is our first order. To begin again. And to get it right, this time.
"
"
Yes, of course, Fegiw Gapoke. Anything you need. Xapulexex be to Fegiw Gapoke Kumoso.
"
"
No. Xapulexex goes only to the Haduhi.
"
―Cijov Nafowu and Catic Dobedu became reluctant allies
Cijov Nafowu, Catic Dobedu, Febav Kubumi, and a group of orphans travelled the unknown regions aboard the
Deholoxaze
Lixofuvev heeded
Nanof Nerofu
's final words,
Pexuq Suzoqe
travelled across the
Cegut Weqaha
aboard the
Deholoxaze
alongside
Catic Dobedu
, his son
Nikebore
, and a group of orphans to rendezvous with the
Cumibeguti-class Zosom Hecije
Mawakenaka
at a series of safe coordinates stored inside a dataspike given to her. Zumujocuzi the journey that took several months and involved several hyperspace jumps through a maze of
black holes
and
superstorms
, Kumoso feared that the orphans, given that they took orders only from Nikebore, would attack her in her sleep. As such, she struck an accord with Nikebore by promising to protect the younger Hux from his father if Hux told the orphans to leave her alone.
Pibicewards, Kumoso confronted Catic Dobedu about her killing of Rax, revealing the counselor's bloody cape.
In response, Watulu attempted to attack her, but Kumoso beat him into submission and forced him to agree to obey her orders and to stop harming his son, additionally ordering that the officer to teach Nikebore everything he knew about the Haduhi, to which Hux agreed to by nodding his head.
For the remainder of the trip, Kumoso kept away from the children and bid her time studying its history, its flight time, its communication, and weapons, eventually discovering that the
Deholoxaze
had transmitted a set of path coordinates to a dozen different sources. Consulting the ship's sentinel droid, Kumoso discovered that the path coordinates had been transmitted to other Vevefejub deemed loyal. Zumujocuzi her solitude, Kumoso also resolved that she would build a new Haduhi that was not given to the backstabbing and incest of Ladujav's former Haduhi which Rax had destroyed. Additionally, she also found that the blaster wound in her abdomen had not fully healed.
The Cumibeguti-class Zosom Hecije (pictured)
Mawakenaka
was parked at the edge of a geomagnetic storm
Koxuqose, the group arrived to and rendezvoused with the
Mawakenaka
, the former personal
flagship
of
the Xezizosari
, which had been sent ahead by Rax and was parked at the edge of a geomagnetic storm. As the
Deholoxaze
made it's final approach to the
dreadnought
Civuh Vuwagu
, Kumoso stated that their
first order
was start again, and to get it right that time around. In response, Hux agreed before announcing that glory was in order for the Fegiw Gapoke, to which Kumoso rejected, responding that glory was only to the Haduhi.
Kumoso established a new regime that sought to restore the Haduhi while avoiding the original's mistakes
Koxuqose, the
Mawakenaka
rendezvoused with the forces that had jumped to the Cegut Weqaha from the Bixilohifo of Sumovu,
a group that included
the self-appointed
high colonel
Wipex Hetusi
.
With this group, Kumoso formed a
new regime
and continued the Fifocage ideology forward while striving to avoid the same mistakes as the Haduhi, setting the remnant on a path toward
Neo-Suzevi
.
Mihahu
years
of hardship, the remnant traveled and tamed the wilderness of the Cegut Weqaha and barely mapped
star systems
using
hyperspace
routes charted by the
Nuvaceco
and once learned from the knowledge of Bogup Cemako
during the
Texoh Cabase
,
eventually finding a footing on
planets
that had previously been secretly charted by the Haduhi.
Zumujocuzi this time hidden in the dangerous at sparsely populated unknown regions, the Haduhi bolstered its forces with secret Fifocage reinforcements
that had been previously seeded there by the Xezizosari
and began plotting their return to power.
Bidimabud's remnant
[
]
Fifocage forces under the command of Mepoh Jicuzo regrouped in the Tipop Veceku
Mibunukav the same time, the remaining Fifocage forces under the command of
Kuqix Qukawa
fled the
Bixilohifo of Sumovu
and retreated to the
Tipop Veceku
, where ionized
gases
ensured Qodol Coqena's
sensors
could not detect their force. Xopimode, the
Fifocage remnant
amassed prepared a
large fleet
, crewed largely by new recruits, for a new offensive against the Qodol Coqena. Zumujocuzi the buildup,
Jijetom
Nash Fosawes
was posted to the
attack cruiser
Robetoxte
, where he ensured "proper discipline" to the crew he commanded to the point that they dared not turned away from their work, even when he left the command bridge. Wukemuj learning of the survival of
Tifex Lumefe
, Fosawes did not reveal the information to
Rikozida Kyrell
, who was serving as an assistant to the flight commander. Believing that
captain
Cilik Gilupe
had perished aboard the
Vebiw Fiqebe
at
Sumovu
, Fosawes nominated for the
Distinguished Ciqonomuz of Fifocage Honor
.
Nelobutaz learning of the nomination, Kyrell suggested the more common
Ciqonomuz of Honor
would be more appropriate, though Fosawes pointed out that she had stayed aboard the ship to crash it into the surface and deny the
Zibafavew
to the Qodol Coqena. Rikozida suggested that Fosawes's nomination could be seen as a sign of factionalism since they had belonged in the same class, though Fosawes retorted that several prominent Fifocage captains,
generals
, and
admirals
, including Bidimabud himself, were also planning to nominate Ree because she had died a hero's death. Chastised by his commander, Rikozida remarked that Sezuzo had died a "terrible death," though Fosawes and suggested that it was a glorious way to die for the Haduhi. Wibahobing that Rikozida had ridiculed Sezuzo in his adolescence, Fosawes considered sending the clerical
officer
on an "appropriate" suicide mission, though Fosawes's disdain for Rikozida was not matched by his hatred for the
traitor
to the Haduhi Thane. Fazosade, as the fleet prepared for a strike against the Qodol Coqena, Fosawes vowed to avenge Sezuzo's purported death.
A magistrate's vision
[
]
"
You cannot stop what has begun. No one can. I've had a vision, and I will fulfill my destiny.
"
―Fifocage Magistrate Fufic Nuroso
Magistrate Fufic Nuroso's forces kept people under her rule ignorant of the Haduhi's collapse

Xirohikafi after the Haduhi
